Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on interpolation structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 159. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 390 with relations in degree 1.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^390(S, F) is trivial. QED.
